018269:                  f'data-rot="{_ord}" data-w1="{s.get("d_w1",0):.1f}" data-m1="{s.get("d_m1",0):.1f}" '

018329: def gate_coeffs(color):
018330:     """NQ4色 → (E_ind, E_lev)。個別 青100/緑100/黄=保有100%継続だが新規停止(トレール0.70のまま変更なし)/赤0、レバ 青100/緑50/黄0/赤0。
018331:        不明/無判定は安全側で(0,0)＝赤扱い。"""
018332:     return {"Blue": (1.0, 1.0), "Green": (1.0, 0.5),
018333:             "Yellow": (1.0, 0.0), "Red": (0.0, 0.0)}.get(color, (0.0, 0.0))
018334: 
018335: def _sleeve_mult(p, n):
018336:     """前回露出p→今回露出n の月中アクションを倍率で表現。建値は価格でlocalStorage保持なので
018337:        株数を増減してもストップ/トレール表示は崩れない。0から増やす時だけ絶対株数が必要。"""
018338:     if n <= 0:
018339:         return "全部売り" if p > 0 else "なし"
018340:     if p <= 0:
018341:         return "Positionsの株数で新規"
018342:     r = n / p
018343:     if abs(r - 1) < 1e-6:
018344:         return "変更なし"
018345:     if abs(r - 0.5) < 1e-6:
018346:         return "半分売り（×0.5）"
018347:     if abs(r - 2) < 1e-6:
018348:         return "倍に買い増し（×2）"
018349:     return f"×{r:.2g}"

018354:     try:
018355:         fails = []

018359:                      ("NFCI", "金融環境"), ("DTWEXBGS", "広義ドル")]

018361:             d = mc.get(sid) or {}
018362:             if not (d.get("vals") or []):
018363:                 fails.append(lab)
018364:         if not (mkt.get("senti") or {}):
018365:             fails.append("センチメント(F&G)")
018366:         if not mkt.get("net_liq"):
018367:             fails.append("ネット流動性")
018368:         if not fails:
018369:             return ""

018372:         return (f'<div class="dhbanner">⚠ <b>本日取得できなかった指標があります</b>'

018380:     """最上部の『今日の運用』。色→露出→今日の引き金（赤明け>色変化>通常）＋隔週月曜リバランス旗。"""

018186: def _svg_mri(ts):
018187:     """Market-status time series, ~6 months, with regime-zone shading (帯域塗り)."""
018188:     if not ts or len(ts) < 5:
018189:         return ""
018190:     ys = [v for _, v in ts]; last = ys[-1]
018191:     hl = sum(ys[-3:]) / 3 if len(ys) >= 3 else last
018192:     band_lab = mri_band(hl)[0].replace("（過熱・反落注意⚠）", "")
018193:     # zone-shaded custom svg (same geometry as _svg_inner)
018194:     n = len(ys); Wd, Ht, pad = 680, 180, 6
018195:     lo, hi = min(ys), max(ys)
018196:     lo = max(0, lo - 4); hi = min(100, hi + 4)
018197:     rng = (hi - lo) or 1
018198:     def X(i): return pad + i * (Wd - 2*pad) / (n - 1)
018199:     def Y(v): return pad + (1 - (v - lo)/rng) * (Ht - 2*pad)
018200:     zones = [(0, 25, "#ef4444"), (25, 40, "#f97316"), (40, 55, "#64748b"),
018201:              (55, 70, "#22c55e"), (70, 100, "#16a34a")]
018202:     zr = ""
018203:     for z0, z1, zc in zones:
018204:         a, b = max(z0, lo), min(z1, hi)
018205:         if b <= a:
018206:             continue
018207:         zr += (f'<rect x="{pad}" y="{Y(b):.1f}" width="{Wd-2*pad}" '
018208:                f'height="{max(0.0, Y(a)-Y(b)):.1f}" fill="{zc}" opacity="0.07"/>')
018209:     pts = " ".join(f"{X(i):.1f},{Y(v):.1f}" for i, v in enumerate(ys))
018210:     gl = "".join(f'<line x1="{pad}" y1="{Y(g):.1f}" x2="{Wd-pad}" y2="{Y(g):.1f}" stroke="#1c2533" stroke-width="1"/>' 
018211:                  f'<text x="{Wd-pad}" y="{Y(g)-2:.1f}" fill="#8b9bb0" font-size="20" font-weight="600" text-anchor="end">{g}</text>'
018212:                  for g in (25, 40, 55, 70) if lo <= g <= hi)
018213:     svg = (f'<svg viewBox="0 0 {Wd} {Ht}" preserveAspectRatio="none">'
018214:            f'<defs><linearGradient id="mg" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1">'
018215:            f'<stop offset="0" stop-color="#34d399" stop-opacity="0.30"/>'
018216:            f'<stop offset="1" stop-color="#34d399" stop-opacity="0"/></linearGradient></defs>'
018217:            f'{zr}{gl}'
018218:            f'<polyline points="{pts}" fill="none" stroke="#34d399" stroke-width="2"/>'
018219:            f'<circle cx="{X(n-1):.1f}" cy="{Y(ys[-1]):.1f}" r="3.5" fill="#34d399"/></svg>')
018220:     return (f'<div class="card"><div class="chd"><h2>Market Conditions 推移</h2><div class="chd-now" style="color:#7ff0a8"><b>{last:.0f}</b><span>{band_lab}</span></div></div>'
018221:             f'<details class="cxpl"><summary>読み方</summary><div class="cxpl-b">Market Conditionsの推移・{_span_label(ts)}（80 Strong Bull / 65 Bull / 55 Weak Bull / 45 Neutral / 35 Weak Bear / 20 Bear）</div></details>'
018222:             f'<div class="chart">{svg}'
018223:             f'{_date_axis(ts)}</div></div>')

018225: def _sec_rows(recs, tf, topbottom=False):
018226:     rs = [x for x in recs if x.get(tf) is not None and not (isinstance(x[tf], float) and np.isnan(x[tf]))]
018227:     rs.sort(key=lambda x: x[tf], reverse=True)
018228:     def row(i, s):
018229:         cells = ""
018230:         for k in ("d1", "w1", "m1"):
018231:             hl = " hl" if k == tf else ""
018232:             cells += f'<td class="{color_pct(s[k])}{hl}">{fmt_pct(s[k])}</td>'
018233:         return (f'<tr><td class="l mut">{i}</td>'
018234:                 f'<td class="l tk" style="font-size:12px">{_h(s["ja"])}'
018235:                 f'<span class="mut" style="font-size:10px"> {s["tk"]}</span></td>{cells}</tr>')
018236:     if topbottom and len(rs) > 10:
018237:         top, bot = rs[:5], rs[-5:]
018238:         h = lambda lab: f'<tr class="secsub"><td colspan="5" class="l">{lab}</td></tr>'
018239:         return (h("強い") + "".join(row(i + 1, s) for i, s in enumerate(top))
018240:                 + h("弱い") + "".join(row(len(rs) - 4 + i, s) for i, s in enumerate(bot)))
018241:     return "".join(row(i + 1, s) for i, s in enumerate(rs))

018329: def gate_coeffs(color):
018330:     """NQ4色 → (E_ind, E_lev)。個別 青100/緑100/黄=保有100%継続だが新規停止(トレール0.70のまま変更なし)/赤0、レバ 青100/緑50/黄0/赤0。
018331:        不明/無判定は安全側で(0,0)＝赤扱い。"""
018332:     return {"Blue": (1.0, 1.0), "Green": (1.0, 0.5),
018333:             "Yellow": (1.0, 0.0), "Red": (0.0, 0.0)}.get(color, (0.0, 0.0))

018335: def _sleeve_mult(p, n):
018336:     """前回露出p→今回露出n の月中アクションを倍率で表現。建値は価格でlocalStorage保持なので
018337:        株数を増減してもストップ/トレール表示は崩れない。0から増やす時だけ絶対株数が必要。"""
018338:     if n <= 0:
018339:         return "全部売り" if p > 0 else "なし"
018340:     if p <= 0:
018341:         return "Positionsの株数で新規"
018342:     r = n / p
018343:     if abs(r - 1) < 1e-6:
018344:         return "変更なし"
018345:     if abs(r - 0.5) < 1e-6:
018346:         return "半分売り（×0.5）"
018347:     if abs(r - 2) < 1e-6:
018348:         return "倍に買い増し（×2）"
018349:     return f"×{r:.2g}"

010653: def load_market_extras(period="9mo", asof_date=None):
010654:     """小分類テーマETF + QQQE + ^VIX6M をライブ取得（macroキャッシュとは独立・失敗時は空dict）。
010655:        返り値: {ticker: Close系列}。--selftest(オフライン)時はネット取得せず空dictを返す。
010656:        #7 asof_date指定時は取得系列を index<=asof で切り、過去as-of分析に現在のライブ値を混ぜない。"""
010657:     tks = sorted(set([t for t, _ in MICRO_ETFS]
010658:                      + [t for t, _ in GICS_THEMES]
010659:                      + [t for t, _ in STYLE_THEMES]
010660:                      + ["QQQE", "^VIX6M"]))
010661:     out = {}
010662:     if not _net_ok():
010663:         return out
010664:     _cut = pd.Timestamp(asof_date) if asof_date is not None else None
010665:     try:
010666:         import yfinance as yf, warnings as _w
010667:         _w.filterwarnings("ignore")
010668:         df = yf.download(tks, period=period, interval="1d", progress=False, threads=True)
010669:         if df is None or getattr(df, "empty", True):
010670:             return out
010671:         cl = df["Close"] if isinstance(df.columns, pd.MultiIndex) and "Close" in df.columns.get_level_values(0) else df
010672:         for t in tks:
010673:             try:
010674:                 s = cl[t].dropna() if hasattr(cl, "columns") and t in cl.columns else None
010675:                 if s is not None and _cut is not None:
010676:                     try: s = s[pd.to_datetime(s.index) <= _cut]      # #7 as-ofより後のバーを落とす
010677:                     except Exception: pass
010678:                 if s is not None and len(s) >= 22:
010679:                     out[t] = s
010680:             except Exception:
010681:                 pass
010682:     except Exception as e:
010683:         sys.stderr.write(f"[extras] fetch failed: {e}\n")
010684:     return out

010686: def _rank_etfs(close_dict, etf_list, minbars=22):
010687:     """{tk: Close系列} と [(tk,ja)] から d1/w1/m1 と 63/126/189日リターンを計算した recs を返す。"""
010688:     recs = []
010689:     for tk, ja in etf_list:
010690:         c = close_dict.get(tk)
010691:         if c is None:
010692:             continue
010693:         c = c.dropna()
010694:         if len(c) < minbars:
010695:             continue
010696:         last = float(c.iloc[-1])
010697:         def r(d):
010698:             return (last/float(c.iloc[-d-1]) - 1) if len(c) > d else np.nan
010699:         recs.append(dict(tk=tk, ja=ja, d1=r(1), w1=r(5), m1=r(21),
010700:                          ret63=r(63), ret126=r(126), ret189=r(189)))
010701:     return recs

010703: def build_sector_ranks(macro, extras):
010704:     """大分類(11セクター・等ウェイトRSP*=キャッシュ) と 小分類(テーマ=ライブextras) の d1/w1/m1。"""
010705:     macro_close = {tk: macro[tk]["Close"] for tk, _ in SECTOR_ETFS if tk in macro}
010706:     return {"macro": _rank_etfs(macro_close, SECTOR_ETFS),
010707:             "micro": _rank_etfs(extras, MICRO_ETFS)}

010709: # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- breadth + yields
010710: def build_breadth(m):
010711:     """分母は「そのMAを算出できた銘柄」だけ。IPO等でsma200がNaNの銘柄を分母に含めると
010712:        (NaN比較はFalse) 200日線上比率が構造的に過小になるため。
010713:        全銘柄欠損などで分母0のときは0%でなくNone（下流で「—」表示）にしてデータ障害を隠さない。"""
010714:     n = len(m)
010715:     v200 = m["sma200"].notna(); n200 = int(v200.sum())
010716:     v50  = m["sma50"].notna();  n50  = int(v50.sum())
010717:     a200 = int((m.loc[v200, "close"] > m.loc[v200, "sma200"]).sum())
010718:     a50  = int((m.loc[v50,  "close"] > m.loc[v50,  "sma50"]).sum())
010719:     _vc  = m["pchg"].notna()
010720:     adv  = int((m.loc[_vc, "pchg"] > 0).sum())
010721:     dec  = int((m.loc[_vc, "pchg"] < 0).sum())
010722:     unch = int((m.loc[_vc, "pchg"] == 0).sum())
010723:     _addn = adv + dec                                  # 変わらず(unch)は分母に入れない（=上昇/下落の比率）
010724:     v52  = m["dist52"].notna(); n52 = int(v52.sum())
010725:     nh   = int((m.loc[v52, "dist52"] >= -0.005).sum())
010726:     return dict(n=int(n), n200=n200, n50=n50,
010727:                 pa200=(100*a200/n200 if n200 > 0 else None),
010728:                 pa50=(100*a50/n50 if n50 > 0 else None),
010729:                 adv=int(adv), dec=int(dec), unch=int(unch),
010730:                 adpct=(100*adv/_addn if _addn > 0 else None),
010731:                 nh=int(nh), pnh=(100*nh/n52 if n52 > 0 else None))
010732: 
010733: def build_yields(macro):
010734:     """（旧・互換用）yfinance ^TNX/^FVX/^TYX の水準。現在は金利カードには使わず、
010735:        DIAGとフォールバック表示のためだけに残す。金利カード本体は build_rates_card()。"""
010736:     out = {}
010737:     for tk, lab in [("^TNX", "米10年名目"), ("^FVX", "米5年"), ("^TYX", "米30年")]:
010738:         if tk in macro:
010739:             c = macro[tk]["Close"].dropna()
010740:             if len(c) >= 2:
010741:                 out[lab] = dict(y=float(c.iloc[-1]), chg=float(c.iloc[-1] - c.iloc[-2]))
010742:     return out

001536:     # macroは系列ごとに独立処理。1系列の正規化失敗で他系列や未正規化データを巻き込まない（#4）。
001537:     if isinstance(_macro, dict):
001538:         _mn = {}
001539:         for _mk, _mv in _macro.items():
001540:             try:
001541:                 _mn[_mk] = normalize_timeseries(_mv, fail_closed=False, label="macro:" + str(_mk))
001542:             except Exception as _e:
001543:                 sys.stderr.write("[normalize] macro:%s failed, dropping: %r\n" % (_mk, repr(_e)[:80]))
001544:                 _mn[_mk] = None
001545:         _macro = {k: v for k, v in _mn.items() if v is not None and hasattr(v, "index") and len(v) > 0}
001546:     # VIX fear-cycleだけは1990年以降の月間Highが必要。短期macro cacheなら
001547:     # Yahooの専用取得で補完する（offline selftestでは_net_ok()がFalse）。
001548:     if _net_ok():
001549:         _vix_full = _fetch_vix_full_history(_macro.get("^VIX"))
001550:         if _vix_full is not None:
001551:             _macro["^VIX"] = normalize_timeseries(
001552:                 _vix_full, fail_closed=False, label="macro:^VIX-full")
001553:     return names, order, s2i, s2t, e2j, _W, _macro, _cache_asof

001598: def _extract(df, tickers, minbars=30):
001599:     out = {}
001600:     if df is None or getattr(df, "empty", True):
001601:         return out
001602:     multi = isinstance(df.columns, pd.MultiIndex)
001603:     need = ["Open", "High", "Low", "Close", "Volume"]
001604:     for t in tickers:
001605:         try:
001606:             sub = df[t] if multi else df
001607:             if sub is None or sub.dropna(how="all").empty:
001608:                 continue
001609:             if not all(c in sub.columns for c in need):
001610:                 continue
001611:             s = sub[need].dropna(how="all")
001612:             if len(s) < minbars:
001613:                 continue
001614:             out[t] = s
001615:         except Exception:
001616:             continue
001617:     return out
001618: 
001619: def _vix_history_usable(frame, strict=True):
001620:     """VIX長期履歴の合否。strict=Trueは理想（1990-01始点・8000本以上）。
001621:     strict=Falseは実運用の下限（MIN_MONTHS分の月数があり起点のズレが許容内）。"""
001622:     try:
001623:         if frame is None or not hasattr(frame, "columns"):
001624:             return False
001625:         if "High" not in frame.columns or "Close" not in frame.columns:
001626:             return False
001627:         idx = frame.dropna(subset=["High", "Close"]).index
001628:         if len(idx) == 0:
001629:             return False
001630:         first = pd.Timestamp(idx.min())
001631:         try:
001632:             first = first.tz_localize(None)
001633:         except TypeError:
001634:             first = first.tz_convert(None)
001635:         if strict:
001636:             return len(frame) >= 8000 and first <= pd.Timestamp("1990-01-05")
001637:         slack = int((first.to_period("M") - VIX_CYCLE_START.to_period("M")).n)
001638:         months = int((pd.Timestamp(idx.max()).tz_localize(None).to_period("M")
001639:                       - first.to_period("M")).n) if len(idx) > 1 else 0
001640:         return slack <= VIX_CYCLE_START_SLACK_MONTHS and months >= VIX_CYCLE_MIN_MONTHS
001641:     except Exception:
001642:         return False
001643: 
001644: 
001645: def _vix_history_complete(frame):
001646:     return _vix_history_usable(frame, strict=True)
001647: 
001648: def _fetch_vix_full_history(existing=None):
001649:     """Yahoo ^VIX daily OHLC from 1990. Existing full history is reused."""
001650:     if _vix_history_complete(existing):
001651:         return existing
001652:     best = existing if _vix_history_usable(existing, strict=False) else None
001653:     if not _net_ok():
001654:         return existing
001655:     try:
001656:         import yfinance as yf
001657:         raw = yf.download("^VIX", start="1990-01-01", progress=False,
001658:                           auto_adjust=False, group_by="ticker", threads=False)
001659:         got = _extract(raw, ["^VIX"], minbars=2500).get("^VIX")
001660:         if _vix_history_complete(got):
001661:             sys.stderr.write("[vix-cycle] ^VIX full history: %d bars\n" % len(got))
001662:             return got
001663:         if _vix_history_usable(got, strict=False):
001664:             sys.stderr.write("[vix-cycle] ^VIX partial history accepted: %d bars\n" % len(got))
001665:             best = got
001666:     except Exception as exc:
001667:         sys.stderr.write("[vix-cycle] batch history failed: %s\n" % type(exc).__name__)
001668:     try:
001669:         hist = yf.Ticker("^VIX").history(start="1990-01-01", auto_adjust=False)
001670:         got = _extract(hist, ["^VIX"], minbars=2500).get("^VIX")
001671:         if _vix_history_complete(got):
001672:             sys.stderr.write("[vix-cycle] ^VIX full history fallback: %d bars\n" % len(got))
001673:             return got
001674:         if best is None and _vix_history_usable(got, strict=False):
001675:             sys.stderr.write("[vix-cycle] ^VIX partial history fallback: %d bars\n" % len(got))
001676:             best = got
001677:     except Exception as exc:
001678:         sys.stderr.write("[vix-cycle] ticker history failed: %s\n" % type(exc).__name__)
001679:     return best if best is not None else existing

001695:     return " ".join(x for x in text.split() if x not in stop)

001684: def _symbol_equiv(a, b):
001685:     import re as _re
001686:     return _re.sub(r"[^A-Z0-9]", "", str(a or "").upper()) == _re.sub(r"[^A-Z0-9]", "", str(b or "").upper())

001688: def _company_name_norm(value):
001689:     """会社名比較専用。法人接尾辞と記号だけを落とし、弱い社名一致をticker変更と誤認しない。"""
001690:     import re as _re
001691:     text = str(value or "").upper().replace("&", " AND ")
001692:     text = _re.sub(r"[^A-Z0-9 ]+", " ", text)
001693:     stop = {"INC", "INCORPORATED", "CORP", "CORPORATION", "CO", "COMPANY", "LTD", "LIMITED",
001694:             "PLC", "LP", "LLC", "SA", "NV", "GROUP", "HOLDING", "HOLDINGS", "THE"}
001695:     return " ".join(x for x in text.split() if x not in stop)
001696: 
001697: def _company_name_score(left, right):
001698:     import difflib
001699:     a, b = _company_name_norm(left), _company_name_norm(right)
001700:     if not a or not b:
001701:         return 0.0
001702:     sa, sb = set(a.split()), set(b.split())
001703:     jac = len(sa & sb) / max(1, len(sa | sb))
001704:     seq = difflib.SequenceMatcher(None, a, b).ratio()
001705:     return max(jac, 0.65 * seq + 0.35 * jac)

001709: def _fmp_get(endpoint, params, api_key, timeout=15):
001710:     """FMP stable API。URL/API keyはログへ出さず、payloadと安全なstatusだけを返す。"""
001711:     if not api_key:
001712:         return None, "no_key"
001713:     if not _net_ok():
001714:         return None, "offline"
001715:     try:
001716:         import urllib.error as _ue
001717:         import urllib.parse as _up
001718:         import urllib.request as _ur
001719:         query = dict(params or {})
001720:         query["apikey"] = api_key
001721:         url = "https://financialmodelingprep.com/stable/%s?%s" % (
001722:             str(endpoint).strip("/"), _up.urlencode(query))
001723:         req = _ur.Request(url, headers={"Accept": "application/json", "User-Agent": "dashboard-builder/1.0"})
--- lines 3662-3846 ---
003662:         out["uni_ad20"] = adv.rolling(20, min_periods=10).mean()
003663:     except Exception as e:
003664:         print("[warn] universe breadth for market score failed:", e)
003665:     return out

003668: def _mc_frame_from_macro(macro):
003669:     cols = {}
003670:     for t in MC_MARKET_TICKERS:
003671:         d = macro.get(t)
003672:         if d is None or not hasattr(d, "columns") or "Close" not in d.columns:
003673:             continue
003674:         x = pd.to_numeric(d["Close"], errors="coerce").dropna()
003675:         if len(x) >= 30:
003676:             cols[t] = x
003677:     if not cols:
003678:         return pd.DataFrame()
003679:     c = pd.concat(cols, axis=1).sort_index()
003680:     try:
003681:         c.index = pd.to_datetime(c.index).tz_localize(None)
003682:     except Exception:
003683:         pass
003684:     return c

003687: def _mc_mean_cols(frame, cols):
003688:     have = [c for c in cols if c in frame.columns]
003689:     if not have:
003690:         return pd.Series(np.nan, index=frame.index)
003691:     return frame[have].mean(axis=1, skipna=True)

003694: def _mc_parent_mean(frame):
003695:     parents = []
003696:     for tickers in MC_INDUSTRY_PARENT.values():
003697:         have = [c for c in tickers if c in frame.columns]
003698:         if have:
003699:             parents.append(frame[have].mean(axis=1, skipna=True))
003700:     return pd.concat(parents, axis=1).mean(axis=1, skipna=True) if parents else pd.Series(np.nan, index=frame.index)

003702: 
003703: def _mc_participation(mask):
003704:     """Equal-weight breadth across every available Market Conditions ETF.
003705: 
003706:     50 means exactly half the available ETFs satisfy the condition. The mapping is
003707:     directionally symmetric: complementing every boolean produces 100-score.
003708:     """
003709:     have = [c for c in MC_MARKET_TICKERS if c in mask.columns]
003710:     if not have:
003711:         return pd.Series(np.nan, index=mask.index)
003712:     return mask[have].mean(axis=1, skipna=True) * 100.0
003713: 
003714: 
003715: def _mc_stratified_median(frame):
003716:     pieces = []
003717:     for cols in (MC_BROAD_ETFS, MC_SECTOR_ETFS):
003718:         have = [c for c in cols if c in frame.columns]
003719:         if have:
003720:             pieces.append(frame[have].median(axis=1, skipna=True))
003721:     parents = []
003722:     for tickers in MC_INDUSTRY_PARENT.values():
003723:         have = [c for c in tickers if c in frame.columns]
003724:         if have:
003725:             parents.append(frame[have].median(axis=1, skipna=True))
003726:     if parents:
003727:         pieces.append(pd.concat(parents, axis=1).mean(axis=1, skipna=True))

003731: def _mc_linear(series, lo, hi):
003732:     return ((pd.to_numeric(series, errors="coerce") - lo) / (hi - lo)).clip(0.0, 1.0) * 100.0

003735: def mri_frame(macro, W=None):
003736:     """Market Conditions v4: 57 ETFs × 12 metrics, fully equal weighted.
003737: 
003738:     No historical percentile recentering and no one-sided deterioration penalty,
003739:     floor or guard. Each 0-100 component has a literal neutral midpoint and the
003740:     final arithmetic mean preserves directional symmetry. EMA2 is smoothing only.
003741:     """
003742:     c = _mc_frame_from_macro(macro)
003743:     if c.empty:
003744:         idx = next((d.index for d in macro.values() if hasattr(d, "index") and len(d.index)), pd.DatetimeIndex([pd.Timestamp.today().normalize()]))
003745:         z = pd.Series(50.0, index=idx, dtype=float)
003746:         vals = pd.DataFrame(index=idx)
003747:         for k in ("pillar_short","pillar_medium","pillar_long","pillar_damage"):
003748:             vals[k] = 50.0
003749:         vals["breadth_level"] = 50.0
003750:         vals["breadth_delta10"] = 0.0
003751:         vals["mc_coverage"] = 0.0
003752:         breakdown = [dict(key=k, w=w, group="market", raw=50.0, pts=w*.5, ptsmax=w, frac=.5)
003753:                      for k,w,*_ in STATUS_DEF]
003754:         return z, breakdown, list(MC_MARKET_TICKERS), list(STATUS_DEF), vals
003755: 
003756:     ma10=c.rolling(10).mean(); ma20=c.rolling(20).mean(); ma50=c.rolling(50).mean(); ma200=c.rolling(200).mean()
003757:     p = {}
003758: 
003759:     # Momentum: fixed horizons only. YTD intentionally excluded because its horizon changes through the year.
003760:     p["ret5"] = _mc_participation((c/c.shift(5)-1 > 0).where(c.shift(5).notna() & c.notna()))
003761:     p["ret21"] = _mc_participation((c/c.shift(21)-1 > 0).where(c.shift(21).notna() & c.notna()))
003762:     p["ret63"] = _mc_participation((c/c.shift(63)-1 > 0).where(c.shift(63).notna() & c.notna()))
003763:     p["ret252"] = _mc_participation((c/c.shift(252)-1 > 0).where(c.shift(252).notna() & c.notna()))
003764: 
003765:     # Breadth.
003766:     p["above10"] = _mc_participation((c > ma10).where(ma10.notna() & c.notna()))
003767:     p["above20"] = _mc_participation((c > ma20).where(ma20.notna() & c.notna()))
003768:     p["above50"] = _mc_participation((c > ma50).where(ma50.notna() & c.notna()))
003769:     p["above200"] = _mc_participation((c > ma200).where(ma200.notna() & c.notna()))
003770: 
003771:     # Trend structure.
003772:     p["ma20_gt_50"] = _mc_participation((ma20 > ma50).where(ma20.notna() & ma50.notna()))
003773:     p["ma50_gt_200"] = _mc_participation((ma50 > ma200).where(ma50.notna() & ma200.notna()))
003774: 
003775:     # Compatibility telemetry retained for existing downstream commentary; not part of the 12-metric score.
003776:     p["ma50_rising"] = _mc_participation((ma50 > ma50.shift(20)).where(ma50.notna() & ma50.shift(20).notna()))
003777: 
003778:     # Damage: score every ETF first, then equal-weight. This avoids a median/stratification tilt.
003779:     hi252 = c.rolling(252,min_periods=200).max()
003780:     dd = c/hi252 - 1.0
003781:     dd_by_etf = ((dd + 0.30) / 0.25 * 100.0).clip(0.0, 100.0)
003782:     p["dd_score"] = dd_by_etf.mean(axis=1, skipna=True)
003783:     p["within10"] = _mc_participation((dd >= -0.10).where(dd.notna()))
003784:     med_dd = dd.median(axis=1, skipna=True)
003785: 
003786:     score_keys = ("ret5","ret21","ret63","ret252",
003787:                   "above10","above20","above50","above200",
003788:                   "ma20_gt_50","ma50_gt_200","dd_score","within10")
003789:     raw = pd.concat([p[k] for k in score_keys], axis=1).mean(axis=1, skipna=True)
003790:     score = raw.ewm(span=2,adjust=False).mean()
003791: 
003792:     # Four display-only summaries; their weights match the number of equal metrics.
003793:     momentum = pd.concat([p[k] for k in ("ret5","ret21","ret63","ret252")],axis=1).mean(axis=1,skipna=True)
003794:     breadth4 = pd.concat([p[k] for k in ("above10","above20","above50","above200")],axis=1).mean(axis=1,skipna=True)
003795:     trend2 = pd.concat([p[k] for k in ("ma20_gt_50","ma50_gt_200")],axis=1).mean(axis=1,skipna=True)
003796:     damage = pd.concat([p[k] for k in ("dd_score","within10")],axis=1).mean(axis=1,skipna=True)
003797: 
003798:     # Breadth momentum is context only, centered on zero and excluded from MC score.
003799:     breadth_level = pd.concat([p["above20"],p["above50"]],axis=1).mean(axis=1,skipna=True)
003800:     breadth_delta10 = breadth_level - breadth_level.shift(10)
003801:     coverage = c.notna().sum(axis=1) / float(len(MC_MARKET_TICKERS)) * 100.0
003802: 
003803:     vals = pd.DataFrame(index=score.index)
003804:     vals["pillar_short"] = momentum; vals["pillar_medium"] = breadth4
003805:     vals["pillar_long"] = trend2; vals["pillar_damage"] = damage
003806:     vals["breadth_level"] = breadth_level; vals["breadth_delta10"] = breadth_delta10
003807:     vals["mc_coverage"] = coverage
003808:     for k,v in p.items(): vals[k] = v
003809:     vals["median_dd"] = med_dd*100.0
003810: 
003811:     def _compat_close(ticker):
003812:         d = macro.get(ticker)
003813:         if d is None or not hasattr(d, "columns") or "Close" not in d.columns:
003814:             return pd.Series(np.nan, index=vals.index, dtype=float)
003815:         x = pd.to_numeric(d["Close"], errors="coerce")
003816:         try:
003817:             x.index = pd.to_datetime(x.index).tz_localize(None)
003818:         except Exception:
003819:             pass
003820:         return x.reindex(vals.index).ffill()
003821: 
003822:     _qqq = _compat_close("QQQ")
003823:     _spy = _compat_close("SPY")
003824:     _rsp = _compat_close("RSP")
003825:     _qqqe = _compat_close("QQQE")
003826:     vals["qqq_50"] = _qqq / _qqq.rolling(50).mean() - 1.0
003827:     vals["qqq_200"] = _qqq / _qqq.rolling(200).mean() - 1.0
003828:     vals["spy_50"] = _spy / _spy.rolling(50).mean() - 1.0
003829:     vals["spy_200"] = _spy / _spy.rolling(200).mean() - 1.0
003830:     vals["rsp_50"] = _rsp / _rsp.rolling(50).mean() - 1.0
003831:     vals["rsp_200"] = _rsp / _rsp.rolling(200).mean() - 1.0
003832:     vals["qqqe_50"] = _qqqe / _qqqe.rolling(50).mean() - 1.0
003833: 
003834:     pmap = {"short":momentum,"medium":breadth4,"long":trend2,"damage":damage}
003835:     breakdown=[]; dropped=[]; active=[]
003836:     for key,w,lo,hi,grp in STATUS_DEF:
003837:         ser=pmap[key]; cur=float(ser.dropna().iloc[-1]) if ser.notna().any() else np.nan
003838:         if not np.isfinite(cur):
003839:             dropped.append(key); continue
003840:         active.append((key,w,lo,hi,grp))
003841:         breakdown.append(dict(key=key,w=w,group=grp,raw=cur,pts=w*cur/100.0,ptsmax=w,frac=cur/100.0))
003842:     return score, breakdown, dropped, active, vals

010486: def _theme_drill_card(themes):
010487:     if not themes:
010488:         return ""
010489:     def tile(i, th):
010490:         rs = th["rs"]; g = rs >= 50
010491:         inten = min(1.0, abs(rs - 50) / 42)
010492:         bg = (f"rgba(34,197,94,{0.14+inten*0.34:.2f})" if g else f"rgba(239,68,68,{0.14+inten*0.34:.2f})")

010496:                 f'<span class="tht-n">{th["name"]}</span>'
010497:                 f'<span class="tht-m"><b>RS{rs:.0f}</b> <span style="color:{acol}">{arr}</span>'
010498:                 f'<span class="tht-c">{th["n"]}銘柄</span></span></button>')
010499:     tiles = "".join(tile(i, th) for i, th in enumerate(themes))
010500:     panels = ""
010501:     for i, th in enumerate(themes):
010502:         chips = "".join(
010503:             f'<span class="thstk" data-tk="{x["t"]}" onclick="thStk(this)">'

010506:             f'{"▲" if x["mom"]>1.5 else "▼" if x["mom"]<-1.5 else "▬"}</span></span>'
010507:             for x in th["members"][:24])
010508:         more = f'<span class="thmore">+{len(th["members"])-24}銘柄</span>' if len(th["members"]) > 24 else ""
010509:         _showtk = [x["t"] for x in th["members"][:24]]
010510:         _alltk = [x["t"] for x in th["members"]]
010511:         panels += (f'<div class="thpanel" id="thp-{i}" style="display:none">'
010512:                    f'<div class="thpanel-h">{th["name"]}・{th["n"]}銘柄 <span class="mut">（RS強い順・タップで詳細）</span>{_cp(_showtk, _alltk)}</div>'
010513:                    f'<div class="thstks">{chips}{more}</div></div>')

010524: def build_sector_etf_rs(macro):
010525:     recs = []
010526:     rets63 = {}
010527:     for tk, ja in SECTOR_ETFS:
010528:         if tk not in macro:
010529:             continue
010530:         c = macro[tk]["Close"].dropna()
010531:         if len(c) < 70:
010532:             continue
010533:         last = float(c.iloc[-1])
010534:         def r(d):
010535:             return (last/float(c.iloc[-d-1])-1) if len(c) > d else np.nan
010536:         rets63[tk] = r(63)
010537:         recs.append(dict(tk=tk, ja=ja, d1=r(1), w1=r(5), m1=r(21), r63=r(63)))
010538:     if not recs:
010539:         return []
010540:     sr = pd.Series({x["tk"]: x["r63"] for x in recs}).rank(pct=True) * 100
010541:     for x in recs:
010542:         x["rs"] = float(sr[x["tk"]])
010543:     recs.sort(key=lambda x: (-(x["r63"] if x["r63"] == x["r63"] else -9)))
010544:     return recs

010653: def load_market_extras(period="9mo", asof_date=None):
010654:     """小分類テーマETF + QQQE + ^VIX6M をライブ取得（macroキャッシュとは独立・失敗時は空dict）。
010655:        返り値: {ticker: Close系列}。--selftest(オフライン)時はネット取得せず空dictを返す。
010656:        #7 asof_date指定時は取得系列を index<=asof で切り、過去as-of分析に現在のライブ値を混ぜない。"""
010657:     tks = sorted(set([t for t, _ in MICRO_ETFS]
010658:                      + [t for t, _ in GICS_THEMES]
010659:                      + [t for t, _ in STYLE_THEMES]
010660:                      + ["QQQE", "^VIX6M"]))
010661:     out = {}
010662:     if not _net_ok():
010663:         return out
010664:     _cut = pd.Timestamp(asof_date) if asof_date is not None else None
010665:     try:
010666:         import yfinance as yf, warnings as _w
010667:         _w.filterwarnings("ignore")
010668:         df = yf.download(tks, period=period, interval="1d", progress=False, threads=True)
010669:         if df is None or getattr(df, "empty", True):
010670:             return out
010671:         cl = df["Close"] if isinstance(df.columns, pd.MultiIndex) and "Close" in df.columns.get_level_values(0) else df
010672:         for t in tks:
010673:             try:
010674:                 s = cl[t].dropna() if hasattr(cl, "columns") and t in cl.columns else None
010675:                 if s is not None and _cut is not None:
010676:                     try: s = s[pd.to_datetime(s.index) <= _cut]      # #7 as-ofより後のバーを落とす
010677:                     except Exception: pass
010678:                 if s is not None and len(s) >= 22:
010679:                     out[t] = s
010680:             except Exception:
010681:                 pass
010682:     except Exception as e:
010683:         sys.stderr.write(f"[extras] fetch failed: {e}\n")
010684:     return out
010685: 
010686: def _rank_etfs(close_dict, etf_list, minbars=22):
010687:     """{tk: Close系列} と [(tk,ja)] から d1/w1/m1 と 63/126/189日リターンを計算した recs を返す。"""
010688:     recs = []
010689:     for tk, ja in etf_list:
010690:         c = close_dict.get(tk)
010691:         if c is None:
010692:             continue
010693:         c = c.dropna()
010694:         if len(c) < minbars:
010695:             continue
010696:         last = float(c.iloc[-1])
010697:         def r(d):
010698:             return (last/float(c.iloc[-d-1]) - 1) if len(c) > d else np.nan
010699:         recs.append(dict(tk=tk, ja=ja, d1=r(1), w1=r(5), m1=r(21),
010700:                          ret63=r(63), ret126=r(126), ret189=r(189)))
010701:     return recs
010702: 
010703: def build_sector_ranks(macro, extras):
010704:     """大分類(11セクター・等ウェイトRSP*=キャッシュ) と 小分類(テーマ=ライブextras) の d1/w1/m1。"""
010705:     macro_close = {tk: macro[tk]["Close"] for tk, _ in SECTOR_ETFS if tk in macro}
010706:     return {"macro": _rank_etfs(macro_close, SECTOR_ETFS),
010707:             "micro": _rank_etfs(extras, MICRO_ETFS)}
010708: 
010709: # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- breadth + yields
010710: def build_breadth(m):
010711:     """分母は「そのMAを算出できた銘柄」だけ。IPO等でsma200がNaNの銘柄を分母に含めると
010712:        (NaN比較はFalse) 200日線上比率が構造的に過小になるため。
010713:        全銘柄欠損などで分母0のときは0%でなくNone（下流で「—」表示）にしてデータ障害を隠さない。"""
010714:     n = len(m)
010715:     v200 = m["sma200"].notna(); n200 = int(v200.sum())
010716:     v50  = m["sma50"].notna();  n50  = int(v50.sum())
010717:     a200 = int((m.loc[v200, "close"] > m.loc[v200, "sma200"]).sum())
010718:     a50  = int((m.loc[v50,  "close"] > m.loc[v50,  "sma50"]).sum())
010719:     _vc  = m["pchg"].notna()
010720:     adv  = int((m.loc[_vc, "pchg"] > 0).sum())
010721:     dec  = int((m.loc[_vc, "pchg"] < 0).sum())
010722:     unch = int((m.loc[_vc, "pchg"] == 0).sum())
010723:     _addn = adv + dec                                  # 変わらず(unch)は分母に入れない（=上昇/下落の比率）
010724:     v52  = m["dist52"].notna(); n52 = int(v52.sum())
010725:     nh   = int((m.loc[v52, "dist52"] >= -0.005).sum())
010726:     return dict(n=int(n), n200=n200, n50=n50,
010727:                 pa200=(100*a200/n200 if n200 > 0 else None),
010728:                 pa50=(100*a50/n50 if n50 > 0 else None),
010729:                 adv=int(adv), dec=int(dec), unch=int(unch),
010730:                 adpct=(100*adv/_addn if _addn > 0 else None),
010731:                 nh=int(nh), pnh=(100*nh/n52 if n52 > 0 else None))
010732: 
010733: def build_yields(macro):
010734:     """（旧・互換用）yfinance ^TNX/^FVX/^TYX の水準。現在は金利カードには使わず、
010735:        DIAGとフォールバック表示のためだけに残す。金利カード本体は build_rates_card()。"""
010736:     out = {}
010737:     for tk, lab in [("^TNX", "米10年名目"), ("^FVX", "米5年"), ("^TYX", "米30年")]:
010738:         if tk in macro:
010739:             c = macro[tk]["Close"].dropna()
010740:             if len(c) >= 2:
010741:                 out[lab] = dict(y=float(c.iloc[-1]), chg=float(c.iloc[-1] - c.iloc[-2]))
010742:     return out

010788: def _fred_cache_path():

010791: def _fred_load_cache():
010792:     try:
010793:         _c = json.load(open(_fred_cache_path()))
010794:     except Exception:
010795:         return {}
010796:     if not isinstance(_c, dict):        # #6 FREDは再取得可能。トップレベルが非dictなら{}にリセット（AttributeError回避）
010797:         sys.stderr.write(f"[fred] cache is not a dict; resetting to empty: {_fred_cache_path()}\n")
010798:         return {}
010799:     return _c

010802:     _defer_cache_write(_fred_cache_path(), cache)   # #6 保留→selftest後にflush
010803:     if True:
010804:         return
010805:     try:
010806:         atomic_write_json(_fred_cache_path(), cache)
010807:     except Exception:

010810: def _fred_parse_csv(text, sid):
010811:     import io as _io
010812:     rows = [r for r in csv.reader(_io.StringIO(text)) if r]
010813:     if not rows:
010814:         return []
010815:     # 列名は 'observation_date','<SID>' 想定だが順番だけで拾う
010816:     out = []
010817:     for r in rows[1:]:
010818:         if len(r) >= 2 and r[1] not in ("", ".", "NaN", "nan"):
010819:             try:
010820:                 out.append((r[0], float(r[1])))
010821:             except Exception:
010822:                 continue
010823:     return out

010520: SECTOR_ETFS = [("RSPT","情報技術"),("RSPF","金融"),("RSPN","資本財"),("RSPD","一般消費財"),
010521:                ("RSPM","素材"),("RSPC","通信"),("RSPU","公益"),("RSPS","生活必需品"),

010524: def build_sector_etf_rs(macro):
010525:     recs = []
010526:     rets63 = {}
010527:     for tk, ja in SECTOR_ETFS:
010528:         if tk not in macro:
010529:             continue
010530:         c = macro[tk]["Close"].dropna()
010531:         if len(c) < 70:
010532:             continue
010533:         last = float(c.iloc[-1])
010534:         def r(d):
010535:             return (last/float(c.iloc[-d-1])-1) if len(c) > d else np.nan
010536:         rets63[tk] = r(63)
010537:         recs.append(dict(tk=tk, ja=ja, d1=r(1), w1=r(5), m1=r(21), r63=r(63)))
010538:     if not recs:
010539:         return []
010540:     sr = pd.Series({x["tk"]: x["r63"] for x in recs}).rank(pct=True) * 100
010541:     for x in recs:
010542:         x["rs"] = float(sr[x["tk"]])
010543:     recs.sort(key=lambda x: (-(x["r63"] if x["r63"] == x["r63"] else -9)))
010544:     return recs

010653: def load_market_extras(period="9mo", asof_date=None):
010654:     """小分類テーマETF + QQQE + ^VIX6M をライブ取得（macroキャッシュとは独立・失敗時は空dict）。
010655:        返り値: {ticker: Close系列}。--selftest(オフライン)時はネット取得せず空dictを返す。
010656:        #7 asof_date指定時は取得系列を index<=asof で切り、過去as-of分析に現在のライブ値を混ぜない。"""
010657:     tks = sorted(set([t for t, _ in MICRO_ETFS]
010658:                      + [t for t, _ in GICS_THEMES]
010659:                      + [t for t, _ in STYLE_THEMES]
010660:                      + ["QQQE", "^VIX6M"]))
010661:     out = {}
010662:     if not _net_ok():
010663:         return out
010664:     _cut = pd.Timestamp(asof_date) if asof_date is not None else None
010665:     try:
010666:         import yfinance as yf, warnings as _w
010667:         _w.filterwarnings("ignore")
010668:         df = yf.download(tks, period=period, interval="1d", progress=False, threads=True)
010669:         if df is None or getattr(df, "empty", True):
010670:             return out
010671:         cl = df["Close"] if isinstance(df.columns, pd.MultiIndex) and "Close" in df.columns.get_level_values(0) else df
010672:         for t in tks:
010673:             try:
010674:                 s = cl[t].dropna() if hasattr(cl, "columns") and t in cl.columns else None
010675:                 if s is not None and _cut is not None:
010676:                     try: s = s[pd.to_datetime(s.index) <= _cut]      # #7 as-ofより後のバーを落とす

010667:         _w.filterwarnings("ignore")
010668:         df = yf.download(tks, period=period, interval="1d", progress=False, threads=True)
010669:         if df is None or getattr(df, "empty", True):
010670:             return out
010671:         cl = df["Close"] if isinstance(df.columns, pd.MultiIndex) and "Close" in df.columns.get_level_values(0) else df
010672:         for t in tks:
010673:             try:
010674:                 s = cl[t].dropna() if hasattr(cl, "columns") and t in cl.columns else None
010675:                 if s is not None and _cut is not None:
010676:                     try: s = s[pd.to_datetime(s.index) <= _cut]      # #7 as-ofより後のバーを落とす
010677:                     except Exception: pass
010678:                 if s is not None and len(s) >= 22:
010679:                     out[t] = s
010680:             except Exception:
010681:                 pass
010682:     except Exception as e:
010683:         sys.stderr.write(f"[extras] fetch failed: {e}\n")
010684:     return out
010685: 
010686: def _rank_etfs(close_dict, etf_list, minbars=22):
010687:     """{tk: Close系列} と [(tk,ja)] から d1/w1/m1 と 63/126/189日リターンを計算した recs を返す。"""
010688:     recs = []
010689:     for tk, ja in etf_list:
010690:         c = close_dict.get(tk)
010691:         if c is None:
010692:             continue
010693:         c = c.dropna()
010694:         if len(c) < minbars:
010695:             continue
010696:         last = float(c.iloc[-1])
010697:         def r(d):
010698:             return (last/float(c.iloc[-d-1]) - 1) if len(c) > d else np.nan
010699:         recs.append(dict(tk=tk, ja=ja, d1=r(1), w1=r(5), m1=r(21),
010700:                          ret63=r(63), ret126=r(126), ret189=r(189)))
010701:     return recs
010702: 
010703: def build_sector_ranks(macro, extras):
010704:     """大分類(11セクター・等ウェイトRSP*=キャッシュ) と 小分類(テーマ=ライブextras) の d1/w1/m1。"""
010705:     macro_close = {tk: macro[tk]["Close"] for tk, _ in SECTOR_ETFS if tk in macro}
010706:     return {"macro": _rank_etfs(macro_close, SECTOR_ETFS),
010707:             "micro": _rank_etfs(extras, MICRO_ETFS)}
010708: 
010709: # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- breadth + yields
010710: def build_breadth(m):
010711:     """分母は「そのMAを算出できた銘柄」だけ。IPO等でsma200がNaNの銘柄を分母に含めると
010712:        (NaN比較はFalse) 200日線上比率が構造的に過小になるため。
010713:        全銘柄欠損などで分母0のときは0%でなくNone（下流で「—」表示）にしてデータ障害を隠さない。"""
010714:     n = len(m)
010715:     v200 = m["sma200"].notna(); n200 = int(v200.sum())
010716:     v50  = m["sma50"].notna();  n50  = int(v50.sum())
010717:     a200 = int((m.loc[v200, "close"] > m.loc[v200, "sma200"]).sum())
010718:     a50  = int((m.loc[v50,  "close"] > m.loc[v50,  "sma50"]).sum())
010719:     _vc  = m["pchg"].notna()
010720:     adv  = int((m.loc[_vc, "pchg"] > 0).sum())
010721:     dec  = int((m.loc[_vc, "pchg"] < 0).sum())
010722:     unch = int((m.loc[_vc, "pchg"] == 0).sum())
010723:     _addn = adv + dec                                  # 変わらず(unch)は分母に入れない（=上昇/下落の比率）
010724:     v52  = m["dist52"].notna(); n52 = int(v52.sum())
010725:     nh   = int((m.loc[v52, "dist52"] >= -0.005).sum())
010726:     return dict(n=int(n), n200=n200, n50=n50,
010727:                 pa200=(100*a200/n200 if n200 > 0 else None),
010728:                 pa50=(100*a50/n50 if n50 > 0 else None),
010729:                 adv=int(adv), dec=int(dec), unch=int(unch),
010730:                 adpct=(100*adv/_addn if _addn > 0 else None),
010731:                 nh=int(nh), pnh=(100*nh/n52 if n52 > 0 else None))
010732: 
010733: def build_yields(macro):
010734:     """（旧・互換用）yfinance ^TNX/^FVX/^TYX の水準。現在は金利カードには使わず、
010735:        DIAGとフォールバック表示のためだけに残す。金利カード本体は build_rates_card()。"""
010736:     out = {}
010737:     for tk, lab in [("^TNX", "米10年名目"), ("^FVX", "米5年"), ("^TYX", "米30年")]:
010738:         if tk in macro:
010739:             c = macro[tk]["Close"].dropna()
010740:             if len(c) >= 2:
010741:                 out[lab] = dict(y=float(c.iloc[-1]), chg=float(c.iloc[-1] - c.iloc[-2]))
010742:     return out

006848: def build_lev_env(macro):
006849:     """SOXX(半導体指数)のトレンドゲート状態＋実現ボラ分位。SOXLの新規サイズ判断の材料。
006850:     ※ボラは『可否』でなく『覚悟の目盛り』。強度スロットルも配分昇格しない。"""
006851:     d = macro.get("SOXX")

006853:         return None
006854:     c = d["Close"].dropna()
006855:     if len(c) < 130:
006856:         return None
006857:     ma50 = c.rolling(50).mean(); ma100 = c.rolling(100).mean()
006858:     up = bool(float(ma50.iloc[-1]) > float(ma100.iloc[-1]))
006859:     gap = float(ma50.iloc[-1]) / float(ma100.iloc[-1]) - 1
006860:     diff = (ma50 - ma100)
006861:     streak = 0
006862:     sign = 1 if up else -1
006863:     for v in diff.iloc[::-1]:

006865:             break

006867:             streak += 1
006868:         else:
006869:             break
006870:     r = c.pct_change(fill_method=None)
006871:     vol20 = float((r.rolling(20).std() * np.sqrt(252)).iloc[-1])
006872:     vser = (r.rolling(20).std() * np.sqrt(252)).dropna()
006873:     vpct_ser = _roll_pct(vser, win=504, minp=120)

006877:     elif vpct >= 75:
006878:         size, scls, snote = "薄く（半分目安）", "neg", "荒れ帯。新規は枚数を半分に"

006881:     else:
006882:         size, scls, snote = "フル（配分どおり）", "pos", "静穏。減衰リスクが最も低い帯"
006883:     # SOXL投入帯（MAE実測: SOXXが50MAの+0〜3%上＝初期逆行が最も浅く−25%級の大事故が半減）
006884:     px = float(c.iloc[-1]); m50 = float(ma50.iloc[-1])

006886:     above50 = px > m50

006889:         entry_note = "初期逆行が最も浅い帯。段階投入で1/3ずつ。割れたら即撤退＝深傷回避"
006890:     elif not above50:

003668: def _mc_frame_from_macro(macro):
003669:     cols = {}
003670:     for t in MC_MARKET_TICKERS:
003671:         d = macro.get(t)
003672:         if d is None or not hasattr(d, "columns") or "Close" not in d.columns:
003673:             continue
003674:         x = pd.to_numeric(d["Close"], errors="coerce").dropna()
003675:         if len(x) >= 30:
003676:             cols[t] = x
003677:     if not cols:
003678:         return pd.DataFrame()
003679:     c = pd.concat(cols, axis=1).sort_index()
003680:     try:
003681:         c.index = pd.to_datetime(c.index).tz_localize(None)
003682:     except Exception:
003683:         pass
003684:     return c

003687: def _mc_mean_cols(frame, cols):
003688:     have = [c for c in cols if c in frame.columns]
003689:     if not have:
003690:         return pd.Series(np.nan, index=frame.index)
003691:     return frame[have].mean(axis=1, skipna=True)

003694: def _mc_parent_mean(frame):
003695:     parents = []
003696:     for tickers in MC_INDUSTRY_PARENT.values():
003697:         have = [c for c in tickers if c in frame.columns]
003698:         if have:
003699:             parents.append(frame[have].mean(axis=1, skipna=True))
003700:     return pd.concat(parents, axis=1).mean(axis=1, skipna=True) if parents else pd.Series(np.nan, index=frame.index)

003702: 
003703: def _mc_participation(mask):
003704:     """Equal-weight breadth across every available Market Conditions ETF.
003705: 
003706:     50 means exactly half the available ETFs satisfy the condition. The mapping is
003707:     directionally symmetric: complementing every boolean produces 100-score.
003708:     """
003709:     have = [c for c in MC_MARKET_TICKERS if c in mask.columns]
003710:     if not have:
003711:         return pd.Series(np.nan, index=mask.index)
003712:     return mask[have].mean(axis=1, skipna=True) * 100.0
003713: 
003714: 
003715: def _mc_stratified_median(frame):
003716:     pieces = []
003717:     for cols in (MC_BROAD_ETFS, MC_SECTOR_ETFS):
003718:         have = [c for c in cols if c in frame.columns]
003719:         if have:
003720:             pieces.append(frame[have].median(axis=1, skipna=True))
003721:     parents = []
003722:     for tickers in MC_INDUSTRY_PARENT.values():
003723:         have = [c for c in tickers if c in frame.columns]
003724:         if have:
003725:             parents.append(frame[have].median(axis=1, skipna=True))
003726:     if parents:
003727:         pieces.append(pd.concat(parents, axis=1).mean(axis=1, skipna=True))
003728:     return pd.concat(pieces, axis=1).mean(axis=1, skipna=True) if pieces else pd.Series(np.nan, index=frame.index)

003731: def _mc_linear(series, lo, hi):
003732:     return ((pd.to_numeric(series, errors="coerce") - lo) / (hi - lo)).clip(0.0, 1.0) * 100.0

003735: def mri_frame(macro, W=None):
003736:     """Market Conditions v4: 57 ETFs × 12 metrics, fully equal weighted.
003737: 
003738:     No historical percentile recentering and no one-sided deterioration penalty,
003739:     floor or guard. Each 0-100 component has a literal neutral midpoint and the
003740:     final arithmetic mean preserves directional symmetry. EMA2 is smoothing only.
003741:     """
003742:     c = _mc_frame_from_macro(macro)
003743:     if c.empty:
003744:         idx = next((d.index for d in macro.values() if hasattr(d, "index") and len(d.index)), pd.DatetimeIndex([pd.Timestamp.today().normalize()]))
003745:         z = pd.Series(50.0, index=idx, dtype=float)
003746:         vals = pd.DataFrame(index=idx)
003747:         for k in ("pillar_short","pillar_medium","pillar_long","pillar_damage"):
003748:             vals[k] = 50.0
003749:         vals["breadth_level"] = 50.0
003750:         vals["breadth_delta10"] = 0.0
003751:         vals["mc_coverage"] = 0.0
003752:         breakdown = [dict(key=k, w=w, group="market", raw=50.0, pts=w*.5, ptsmax=w, frac=.5)
003753:                      for k,w,*_ in STATUS_DEF]
003754:         return z, breakdown, list(MC_MARKET_TICKERS), list(STATUS_DEF), vals
003755: 
003756:     ma10=c.rolling(10).mean(); ma20=c.rolling(20).mean(); ma50=c.rolling(50).mean(); ma200=c.rolling(200).mean()
003757:     p = {}
003758: 
003759:     # Momentum: fixed horizons only. YTD intentionally excluded because its horizon changes through the year.
003760:     p["ret5"] = _mc_participation((c/c.shift(5)-1 > 0).where(c.shift(5).notna() & c.notna()))
003761:     p["ret21"] = _mc_participation((c/c.shift(21)-1 > 0).where(c.shift(21).notna() & c.notna()))

007223: def _ov_fs_patch(tpl):
007224:     """フルスクリーン時に画面いっぱいに表示されるようレイアウトを補正する。
007225:     - body背景を濃色→カードと同じベージュにし、余白が黒く見えないように
007226:     - カードを画面中央に配置
007227:     - 縦画面ではカードを90°回転して画面を最大活用、横画面は高さに合わせて最大化
007228:     - 埋め込みタブ（aspect-ratio付きiframe）では横比率のため回転せず従来通りぴったり表示
007229:     """
007230:     # body: 濃色→ベージュ・全高フレックス中央・パディング撤去
007231:     tpl = _re2.sub(
007232:         r'body\{background:#111014;[^}]*\}',
007233:         "html{height:100%}body{background:#E9E7DF;font-family:-apple-system,'Helvetica Neue',Arial,"
007234:         "'Hiragino Sans','Noto Sans JP',sans-serif;margin:0;height:100%;display:flex;align-items:center;"
007235:         "justify-content:center;overflow:hidden;color:#1B1D1C}", tpl, count=1)
007236:     # stage: 全高フレックス中央
007237:     tpl = _re2.sub(r'\.stage\{[^}]*\}',
007238:                    ".stage{width:100%;height:100%;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;overflow:hidden}",
007239:                    tpl, count=1)
007240:     # scaler: 変形基準を中央へ
007241:     tpl = _re2.sub(r'\.scaler\{transform-origin:[^}]*\}',
007242:                    ".scaler{transform-origin:center center}", tpl, count=1)
007243:     # fit(): contain＋縦回転
007244:     tpl = _re2.sub(
007245:         r'function fit\(\)\{var stage=document\.getElementById\(.stage.\),scaler=document\.getElementById\(.scaler.\);[^}]*\}',
007246:         "function fit(){var scaler=document.getElementById('scaler');var W=window.innerWidth,H=window.innerHeight,"
007247:         "cw=1680,ch=1080;if(H>W){var sc=Math.min(W/ch,H/cw);scaler.style.transform='rotate(90deg) scale('+sc+')';}"
007248:         "else{var sc=Math.min(W/cw,H/ch);scaler.style.transform='scale('+sc+')';}"
007249:         "scaler.style.width=cw+'px';scaler.style.height=ch+'px';if(typeof render==='function'){render();}}", tpl, count=1)
007250:     # Publish内のミニチャートは欠損系列を描画しない。空配列のまま path を作ると
007251:     # Chromiumで d="M" / d="M L..." となり、コンソールエラーと書式崩れの原因になる。

007253:     tpl = tpl.replace(
007254:         "function line(vals,w,h,stroke,fillOp){var mn=",
007255:         "function line(vals,w,h,stroke,fillOp){if(!Array.isArray(vals)||vals.length<2)return " + _empty_chart + ";var mn=",
007256:         1)
007257:     tpl = tpl.replace(
007258:         "function lineDated(vals,w,h,stroke,fillOp,labels){var mn=",
007259:         "function lineDated(vals,w,h,stroke,fillOp,labels){if(!Array.isArray(vals)||vals.length<2)return " + _empty_chart + ";labels=Array.isArray(labels)?labels:[];var mn=",
007260:         1)
007261:     tpl = tpl.replace(
007262:         "function multiline(series,w,h,pad){pad=pad||0.10;var all=[];series.forEach(function(s){all=all.concat(s.vals);});",
007263:         "function multiline(series,w,h,pad){series=(Array.isArray(series)?series:[]).filter(function(s){return s&&Array.isArray(s.vals)&&s.vals.length>=2;});if(!series.length)return " + _empty_chart + ";pad=pad||0.10;var all=[];series.forEach(function(s){all=all.concat(s.vals);});",

007303: def _ov_fold_patch(tpl):
007304:     """本体の foldCalc と同じ発想で、冗長な説明文（コメント/判定/副題/フロー解説）を
007305:     既定で閉じた <details> に折りたたむ。スクショ時は小さなトグルだけ残り本文は非表示、
007306:     タップで展開できる。結論値・チャート・ランキングは常時表示のまま。"""
007307:     css = ("details.ovfold{margin:8px 0 0}"
007308:            "details.ovfold>summary{list-style:none;cursor:pointer;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;"
007309:            "gap:5px;font-size:12px;font-weight:700;color:var(--mut);opacity:.68;user-select:none;padding:2px 0}"
007310:            "details.ovfold>summary::-webkit-details-marker{display:none}"
007311:            "details.ovfold>summary::before{content:'\\25B8';font-size:9px}"
007312:            "details.ovfold[open]>summary::before{content:'\\25BE'}"
007313:            "details.ovfold[open]>summary{margin-bottom:5px}"
007314:            "details.ovfold:not([open])>*:not(summary){display:none!important}")
007315:     js = ("<script>function ovFold(){try{"
007316:           "function mk(label){var d=document.createElement('details');d.className='ovfold';"
007317:           "var s=document.createElement('summary');s.textContent=label;d.appendChild(s);return d;}"
007318:           # 単体（判定/副題/フロー解説）。コメントは常時表示のため畳まない。
007319:           "var L={'fnote':'判定','read':'解説','flownote':'フロー解説'};"
007320:           "['fnote','read','flownote'].forEach(function(cl){"
007321:           "Array.prototype.forEach.call(document.getElementsByClassName(cl),function(el){"
007322:           "if(el.closest('details.ovfold'))return;var d=mk(L[cl]||'詳しく');"
007323:           "el.parentNode.insertBefore(d,el);d.appendChild(el);});});"
007324:           "}catch(e){}}"
007325:           "if(document.readyState!=='loading'){setTimeout(ovFold,90);}"
007326:           "else{document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',function(){setTimeout(ovFold,90);});}</script>")
007327:     if "</style>" in tpl:
007328:         tpl = tpl.replace("</style>", css + "</style>", 1)
007329:     if "</body>" in tpl:
007330:         tpl = tpl.replace("</body>", js + "</body>", 1)
007331:     else:
007332:         tpl = tpl + js
007333:     return tpl

008340: # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- setups (D, all chips)
008341: def build_setups(m):
008342:     up200 = m["close"] > m["sma200"]
008343:     up50  = m["close"] > m["sma50"]
008344:     def names(mask, sort="rs"):
008345:         ok = mask.fillna(False)
008346:         if "selection_excluded" in m.columns:
008347:             ok = ok & (~m["selection_excluded"].fillna(False))
008348:         sub = m[ok].sort_values(sort, ascending=False)
008349:         return list(sub.index)
008350:     setups = {}
008351:     setups["押し目"] = names(up200 & up50 & (m["rs"] >= 88) &
008352:                            ((m["close"] / m["ema21"] - 1).abs() <= 0.025))
008353:     setups["ブレイク近"] = names((m["stage2"] == True) & (m["rs189"] >= 85)
008354:                               & (m["pivot_dist"] >= -0.03) & (m["pivot_dist"] <= 0.005), "vcp_score")
008355:     setups["出来高急増"] = names(up50 & (m["pchg"] > 0) & (m["volx"] >= 2.5) & (m["rs"] >= 85))
008356:     setups["モメンタム"] = names(up50 & (m["rs"] >= 92) & (m["ret21"] >= 0.15))
008357:     setups["ボラ収縮"] = names((m["vcp_ready"] == True) & (m["rs189"] >= 80), "vcp_score")
008358:     setups["深押し"] = names(up200 & (m["dist52"] <= -0.15) & (m["rs"] >= 75))
008359:     return setups

008363: def build_structure_scanner(m, cap=10):
008364:     """Compact structural scanner: confirmed breakout, VCP, cup/handle, trend change and 20/4."""
008365:     liq = (m["close"] >= 5) & (m["dvol"] >= DVOL_FLOOR)
008366:     leader = (m["rs189"] >= 80)
008367:     specs = [
008368:         ("真のブレイク", m[(m["true_breakout"] == True) & liq & leader], "breakout_score"),
008369:         ("VCP準備", m[(m["vcp_ready"] == True) & (m["true_breakout"] != True) & liq & leader], "vcp_score"),
008370:         ("カップ・ハンドル", m[((m["cup_ready"] == True) | (m["cup_breakout"] == True)) & liq & leader], "rs189"),
008371:         ("転換確認", m[(m["trend_change"] == True) & (m["true_breakout"] != True)
008372:                          & (m["cup_breakout"] != True) & liq & (m["rs189"] >= 70)], "rs189"),
008373:         ("20/4進行", m[(m["power20_4"] == True) & liq & leader], "brk_ret"),
008374:         ("ブレイク失敗", m[(m["breakout_failure"] == True) & liq], "brk_ret"),
008375:     ]
008376:     blocks = []
008377:     for name, sub, sortcol in specs:
008378:         if sub.empty:
008379:             chips = '<span class="empty">該当なし</span>'
008380:             n = 0
008381:         else:
008382:             sub = sub.sort_values(sortcol, ascending=False).head(cap)
008383:             n = len(sub)
008384:             cc = []
008385:             for t, r in sub.iterrows():
008386:                 if name == "真のブレイク":
008387:                     meta = f'RVOL {float(r.get("rvol_pre")):.1f}×' if pd.notna(r.get("rvol_pre")) else ''
008388:                 elif name == "VCP準備":
008389:                     meta = f'質 {int(round(float(r.get("vcp_score", 0))))}'
008390:                 elif name == "カップ・ハンドル":
008391:                     meta = f'深さ {float(r.get("cup_depth"))*100:.0f}%' if pd.notna(r.get("cup_depth")) else ''
008392:                 elif name == "転換確認":
008393:                     meta = f'RS {float(r.get("rs189")):.0f}' if pd.notna(r.get("rs189")) else ''
008394:                 elif name == "20/4進行":
008395:                     meta = f'+{float(r.get("brk_ret"))*100:.0f}%/{int(r.get("brk_days"))}日' if pd.notna(r.get("brk_ret")) and pd.notna(r.get("brk_days")) else ''
008396:                 else:
008397:                     meta = f'{float(r.get("brk_ret"))*100:.1f}%/{int(r.get("brk_days"))}日' if pd.notna(r.get("brk_ret")) and pd.notna(r.get("brk_days")) else ''
008398:                 _pv = float(r.get("pivot40")) if pd.notna(r.get("pivot40")) else float("nan")
008399:                 _pd = float(r.get("pivot_dist")) if pd.notna(r.get("pivot_dist")) else float("nan")
008400:                 title = (f'ピボット {_pv:.2f} / 距離 {_pd*100:+.1f}% / '
008401:                          f'収縮 {int(r.get("contractions") or 0)} / 安値切上げ {"○" if bool(r.get("higher_lows")) else "—"}')
008402:                 cc.append(f'<span class="chip hot" title="{title}" data-liq="{(r.get("dvol") or 0)/1e6:.1f}" data-tkone="{t}"><b>{t}</b><span class="mut" style="font-size:10px">{meta}</span></span>')
008403:             chips = ''.join(cc)
008404:         blocks.append(f'<div class="patblk"><div class="patname">{name}<span class="ct">{n}</span></div><div class="chips">{chips}</div></div>')
008405:     return ('<div class="card"><h2>構造スキャナー</h2>'

012675: def _market_comment(aux, mkt, sar, breadth, cat_html=""):
012676:     """Market opening narrative: readable in about 20 seconds and directly reusable on X.
012677: 
012678:     The visible block carries the story. A native <details> panel keeps the supporting
012679:     diagnostics available without taking permanent vertical space.
012680:     """
012681:     def finite(v):
012682:         try: return v is not None and np.isfinite(float(v))
012683:         except Exception: return False
012684:     def ts_delta(ts, lag):
012685:         vals = [float(v) for _, v in (ts or []) if finite(v)]
012686:         return (vals[-1] - vals[-1-lag]) if len(vals) > lag else None
012687:     def dnum(v, suffix=""):
012688:         return "—" if not finite(v) else f"{float(v):+.0f}{suffix}"
012689:     def dpct(v):
012690:         return "—" if not finite(v) else f"{float(v)*100:+.1f}%"
012691:     def dirof(ts, up, dn):
012692:         if not ts or len(ts) < 60: return None
012693:         st, _ = _trend_state([v for _, v in ts], up, dn)
012694:         return "横ばい" if st == "中立" else st
012695:     def pick_index(prefix):
012696:         for x in ((mkt.get("perf") or {}).get("indices") or []):
012697:             if str(x.get("label", "")).startswith(prefix): return x
012698:         return {}
012699:     def rank_one(recs, key, top=True):
012700:         arr = [x for x in (recs or []) if finite(x.get(key))]
012701:         if not arr: return None
012702:         arr.sort(key=lambda x: float(x[key]), reverse=top)
012703:         return arr[0]
012704:     def lead_text(rec, key):
012705:         if not rec: return "—"
012706:         return f'{rec.get("ja") or rec.get("label") or rec.get("tk") or "—"} {dpct(rec.get(key))}'
012707:     def period_sector(key):
012708:         sr = mkt.get("sector_ranks") or {}
012709:         mac = sr.get("macro") or []
012710:         mic = sr.get("micro") or []
012711:         tm = rank_one(mac, key, True); tn = rank_one(mic, key, True)
012712:         wm = rank_one(mac, key, False); wn = rank_one(mic, key, False)
012713:         tops = [lead_text(x, key) for x in (tm, tn) if x]
012714:         weaks = [lead_text(x, key) for x in (wm, wn) if x]
012715:         return ("・".join(tops) if tops else "—", "・".join(weaks) if weaks else "—")
012716:     def names(xs, n=2):
012717:         return "・".join(str(x.get("ja") or x.get("sub") or x.get("tk")) for x in xs[:n]) if xs else "—"
012718:

006382:     def _pack(name, idxs, thin=False):
006383:         sub = m.loc[idxs]
006384:         rs = sub["rs189"].dropna()
006385:         if rs.empty:
006386:             return None
006387:         x = float(rs.median())
006388:         y = 0.0
006389:         if lag_col:
006390:             prev = sub[lag_col].dropna()
006391:             if len(prev) >= max(3, min_n // 2):
006392:                 y = x - float(prev.median())
006393:         strong = float((rs >= SECTOR_BREADTH_RS).mean() * 100.0)
006394:         q = ("主導" if (x >= SECTOR_LEAD_RS and y >= 0) else
006395:              "弱化" if x >= SECTOR_LEAD_RS else
006396:              "改善" if y >= 0 else "停滞")
006397:         top = sub["rs189"].sort_values(ascending=False).head(12)
006398:         members = [dict(t=t, rs=int(round(float(v)))) for t, v in top.items() if v == v]
006399:         return dict(sub=name, q=q, rs=int(round(x)), drs=int(round(y)),
006400:                     n=len(idxs), br=int(round(strong)), thin=bool(thin),
006401:                     members=members)

006435: def build_rrg_etf(macro, extras, win=63, mom=10, themes=None):
006436:     """【大枠・市場全体】テーマETFのSPY相対力(63日基準)×その10日モメンタムで4象限。
006437:        市場全体のセクター資金フローを見る古典的RRG。themes未指定なら主要18テーマ(RRG_THEMES)。"""
006438:     b = macro.get("SPY")
006439:     if b is None:
006440:         return []
006441:     bc = b["Close"].dropna()
006442:     pts = []
006443:     for tk, ja in (themes if themes is not None else RRG_THEMES):
006444:         sc = extras.get(tk)
006445:         if sc is None:
006446:             d = macro.get(tk); sc = d["Close"] if d is not None else None
006447:         if sc is None:
006448:             continue
006449:         sc = sc.dropna()
006450:         r = (sc / bc).dropna()
006451:         if len(r) < win + mom + 15:
006452:             continue
006453:         ratio = (100 * r / r.rolling(win).mean()).dropna()
006454:         if len(ratio) < mom + 12:
006455:             continue
006456:         mser = (ratio / ratio.shift(mom) - 1) * 100
006457:         def _snap(lag):
006458:             if len(ratio) <= lag or len(mser) <= lag:
006459:                 return None
006460:             xx, yy = float(ratio.iloc[-1 - lag]), float(mser.iloc[-1 - lag])

003703: def _mc_participation(mask):
003704:     """Equal-weight breadth across every available Market Conditions ETF.
003705: 
003706:     50 means exactly half the available ETFs satisfy the condition. The mapping is
003707:     directionally symmetric: complementing every boolean produces 100-score.
003708:     """
003709:     have = [c for c in MC_MARKET_TICKERS if c in mask.columns]
003710:     if not have:
003711:         return pd.Series(np.nan, index=mask.index)
003712:     return mask[have].mean(axis=1, skipna=True) * 100.0

003715: def _mc_stratified_median(frame):
003716:     pieces = []
003717:     for cols in (MC_BROAD_ETFS, MC_SECTOR_ETFS):
003718:         have = [c for c in cols if c in frame.columns]
003719:         if have:
003720:             pieces.append(frame[have].median(axis=1, skipna=True))
003721:     parents = []
003722:     for tickers in MC_INDUSTRY_PARENT.values():
003723:         have = [c for c in tickers if c in frame.columns]
003724:         if have:
003725:             parents.append(frame[have].median(axis=1, skipna=True))
003726:     if parents:
003727:         pieces.append(pd.concat(parents, axis=1).mean(axis=1, skipna=True))
003728:     return pd.concat(pieces, axis=1).mean(axis=1, skipna=True) if pieces else pd.Series(np.nan, index=frame.index)

003732:     return ((pd.to_numeric(series, errors="coerce") - lo) / (hi - lo)).clip(0.0, 1.0) * 100.0

003895: def mri_band(v):
003896:     if v >= 80: return ("STRONG BULL", "bull")
003897:     if v >= 65: return ("BULL", "bull")
003898:     if v >= 55: return ("WEAK BULL", "bull")

003900:     if v >= 35: return ("WEAK BEAR", "weak")
003901:     if v >= 20: return ("BEAR", "bear")
003902:     return ("STRONG BEAR", "bear")

003934: def _nq_stable_color_series(df, scol_name, ts, cut):
003935:     """NQ export DFから色系列を返す。仕様: ts昇順に並べ、同一時刻はCSV上の最終行を正式値とする(keep=last)。
003936:        日付の降順/昇順には依存しないが、同一時刻に別の状態IDが競合する場合は元ファイルの行順を優先する
003937:        （完全な行順非依存にするには別途どの状態を優先するかの正式ルールが必要）。競合があれば警告する。"""
003938:     tmp = pd.DataFrame({"ts": ts, "sid": pd.to_numeric(df[scol_name], errors="coerce"),
003939:                         "_row": np.arange(len(df))})
003940:     tmp = tmp.dropna(subset=["ts", "sid"])
003941:     if cut is not None:
003942:         tmp = tmp.loc[tmp["ts"] <= cut]
003943:     try:
003944:         if tmp.groupby("ts")["sid"].nunique().gt(1).any():
003945:             sys.stderr.write("[nq] same timestamp has conflicting state IDs; using last row per timestamp\n")
003946:     except Exception:
003947:         pass
003948:     tmp = tmp.sort_values(["ts", "_row"], kind="mergesort").drop_duplicates("ts", keep="last")
003949:     return [c for c in (_SAR_ID2COLOR.get(int(v)) for v in tmp["sid"]) if c]

003851:         d = ma10.iloc[-1] - ma10.iloc[-3]

003855:         ("モメンタム", last.get("pillar_short", np.nan) < 40.0),
003856:         ("広がり", last.get("pillar_medium", np.nan) < 40.0),
003857:         ("トレンド", last.get("pillar_long", np.nan) < 40.0),
003858:         ("Damage", last.get("pillar_damage", np.nan) < 40.0),
003859:     ]
003860:     bear_flags = [(lab, bool(v == True)) for lab,v in bvals]
003861:     bear_n = int(sum(1 for _,v in bear_flags if v))

003863:     hi20 = float(clean.tail(20).max()) if len(clean) else cur
003864:     drop = hi20-cur
003865:     if drop < 3:    peak = "通常"
003866:     elif drop < 7:  peak = "注意"
003867:     elif drop < 12: peak = "減速"
003868:     else:           peak = "深押し"
003869:     _bd10 = last.get("breadth_delta10", np.nan)
003870:     try:
003871:         breadth_delta10 = float(_bd10) if _bd10 is not None and np.isfinite(float(_bd10)) else 0.0
003872:     except Exception:
003873:         breadth_delta10 = 0.0
003874:     breadth_arrow = "↑" if breadth_delta10 > 2.0 else ("↓" if breadth_delta10 < -2.0 else "→")
003875:     _component_keys = ("ret5","ret21","ret63","ret252",
003876:                        "above10","above20","above50","above200",
003877:                        "ma20_gt_50","ma50_gt_200","dd_score","within10")

003880:         try:
003881:             _v = float(last.get(_k, np.nan))
003882:             components[_k] = _v if np.isfinite(_v) else None
003883:         except Exception:
003884:             components[_k] = None
003885:     try:
003886:         mc_coverage = float(last.get("mc_coverage", np.nan))
003887:     except Exception:
003888:         mc_coverage = np.nan

004013: def _psar(h, l, c, step=0.02, mx=0.20):
004014:     """Wilder Parabolic SAR (AF 0.02 step, 0.20 max)."""
004015:     n = len(c); sar = np.zeros(n); bull = True; af = step; ep = l[0]; sar[0] = l[0]
004016:     for i in range(1, n):
004017:         sar[i] = sar[i-1] + af*(ep - sar[i-1])
004018:         if bull:
004019:             if l[i] < sar[i]:
004020:                 bull = False; sar[i] = ep; ep = l[i]; af = step
